# Rules Test Inventory

This working document supports ADR-0032. It preserves the earlier claim-level
inventory, and adds a current locator for each historical row. The inventory is
not a fresh exhaustive audit of the present codebase. In particular, tests added
or changed since that audit, including Totem matrices, require new claim-level
review before any existing evidence is removed.

The historical test location, dispositions, decisions, and recorded test runs
are retained as audit history, not as current implementation or validation
claims. A current locator means only that the same function name (or Playwright
test title) was found; it does not establish that its assertions still prove
the historical claim. Missing or ambiguous locators require manual reconciliation,
not an assumption that evidence was deleted. Old line numbers and commands in
historical columns refer to the earlier layout. The current Rust integration
suites live under `tests/single/` and run through `cargo test --test single`.
See [the current follow-up plan](rule-test-follow-ups.md) and
[the codebase checkpoint](refactor-result.md). One audit owner should maintain
this inventory and review each batch with the primary implementer.

No existing test may be removed until every unique claim it proves has named,
passing replacement evidence. Confirmed product defects may be fixed before the inventory is
complete, but those fixes do not authorize early test deletion.

## Classification vocabulary

- `baseline`: an unmodified rule effect
- `interaction`: two or more rules meet at one resolution boundary
- `invariant`: a state or engine condition that must always hold
- `contract`: matcher, catalog, serialization, error, or other narrow boundary
- `replay`: canonical replay or replay verification
- `public-view`: viewer filtering or Player-visible projection
- `web-seam`: representative browser-to-Rules-Engine integration

## Disposition vocabulary

- `retain`: the claim remains best proved by this test
- `merge`: move the claim into a named interaction matrix
- `rewrite`: keep the claim but replace its evidence surface
- `delete`: every claim has named replacement evidence
- `unresolved`: audit owner and primary implementer must decide before continuing

## Audit protocol

For each batch, the audit owner reports:

1. Tests inspected and every unique claim extracted from them.
2. Proposed disposition and replacement matrix for each claim.
3. Reusable scenario builders or fixtures.
4. Cases that do not fit the current classification rules.
5. Proposed changes to the audit criteria themselves.

The primary implementer records each ruling in the decision column before the
audit owner starts the next batch.

## Historical inventory with current locators

Use one row per unique claim; repeat a test when it proves several claims.

- `src/web_api.rs:card_movement_summary_names_only_cards_visible_to_the_viewer` — mirrored human-language rendering; canonical card redaction is retained in `src/public_view.rs` above.
- `src/web_api.rs:hidden_card_refs_use_plain_player_facing_copy` — presentation copy only; hidden-count shape is already retained.
- `src/web_api.rs:turn_draw_records_use_rulebook_terms` — localized event wording only; canonical Turn Draw behavior and public event contracts are separately retained.
- `src/web_api.rs:attack_record_reports_damage_to_the_players_shield` — localized event wording only; canonical Shield resolution is matrix evidence in Batch 16.
- `src/web_api.rs:empty_city_record_only_states_that_it_flipped` — localized event wording only; Empty City passive behavior is Batch 2 evidence.
- `src/web_api.rs:inspected_hand_presentation_only_lists_cards_for_the_authorized_view` — mirrored text rendering; underlying authorized-view redaction is retained in Batch 16 and Batch 17.
- `src/web_api.rs:player_event_unknown_name_fallbacks_are_safe_and_not_no_profession` — localized fallback wording only; catalog vocabulary coverage and canonical-id redaction are retained above.

The remaining `src/web_api.rs` tests already have rows in their owning rule-module batches.

### Batch 18 explicit scope exclusions

- `apps/web/tests/e2e/local-password-reset.spec.ts:an enabled local password reset repairs an email credential and signs the player in` — account/authentication workflow, outside Rules Engine evidence.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/personal-deck.spec.ts:account menu closes when clicking outside it` — account-menu focus/interaction presentation only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/personal-deck.spec.ts:account menu opens the valid built-in personal deck editor` — deck-editor navigation, layout, and local draft presentation; independent deck catalog/validation contracts are retained in Batch 11.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/personal-deck.spec.ts:imports, exports, and rejects malformed personal-deck drafts` — local editor parsing, clipboard, focus, and transport presentation; it is not Rules Engine behavior evidence.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/playable-actions.spec.ts:selected cards expose rule-backed actions in vertically ordered control panels` — action-panel layout, processing/error copy, and command-envelope UI; typed Rust action-detail/query contracts are retained in Batches 16–17.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/discard-composition.spec.ts:players can inspect a synchronized discard composition throughout a match` — discard-dialog layout, focus, synchronization display, and responsive presentation; canonical Card movement and confidentiality are retained in Batches 16–17.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/star-rule-module.spec.ts:disabling Star invalidates readiness and locked decks while preserving Base play` — room configuration/readiness and UI module-state lifecycle; Star-disabled behavior and module configuration contracts are retained in Batches 7 and 14.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/star-rule-module.spec.ts:a four-player team room starts with one shared immutable Star configuration` — room/board layout and module-display topology; four-Player rule topology evidence is retained in Batches 7 and 16.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:unauthenticated deep links preserve room invite and replay queries through login` — authentication routing only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:unauthenticated root visitors stay on the public homepage` — authentication/landing routing only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:the root route renders publicly before a signed-in client continues to rooms` — route redirect/lobby lifecycle only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:Deck owns its route-local loading and retryable error outcomes` — route-local fetch/loading/error presentation only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:Deck owns its import dialog focus, cleans up its listener, and Replay routes render their own error outcome` — dialog focus/listener and route-error presentation only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:Lobby exposes its route-local loading state before rooms arrive` — lobby loading presentation only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:Lobby navigation reloads Game state, resets parameter-local state, and tears down the room session before re-entry` — room-session navigation/lifecycle only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/route-lifecycle.spec.ts:leaving a loading Game route cannot recreate its room session` — room-session teardown race only.
- `apps/web/tests/e2e/landing.spec.ts:public homepage presents the game, metadata, and login entry` — public landing, SEO metadata, and link presentation only.
- Reconciliation: all 25 Playwright test cases across the 11 E2E specs are now classified: 8 representative Player-visible Rules Engine seams (the two existing rows in Batches 11 and 15 plus the six newly covered cases above) and 17 explicit scope exclusions. No browser case is primary Rules Engine behavior evidence.
## Cross-batch adjudications

- Every No-Effect or passive No-Effect grounds assertion compares a set of grounds. A deterministic serialized vector order is wire-only and must never assert a domain rule priority.
- Mandatory Hero Schools follow-up: every matrix role is reached through legal `ChangeProfession` commands; no direct profession fixture may stand in for role acquisition.
- Mandatory Hero Schools follow-up: add legal Warrior wood-plus-one Defense-proficiency establishment and consumption evidence.
- Mandatory Hero Schools follow-up: add legal Spirit Mesmer Magic Seal establishment and consumption evidence, including the Benevolent and Golden Cicada grounds-set interaction.

## Completion gate

- Every Rust rules-behavior test has at least one inventory row.
- Every relevant Playwright case is classified as a Web seam or redundant rules
  evidence.
- No row remains `unresolved`.
- Every `merge`, `rewrite`, or `delete` row names replacement evidence.
- Every unique claim scheduled for deletion is covered by an implemented,
  passing replacement case.
- The audit owner and primary implementer have reviewed the final batch and the
  cross-batch classification for consistency.
